tbmm-2021-12-22sit02spe0307par0382	Mr. President, I have spoken on behalf of the National Movement Party Group about the first chapter of the First Amendment to the Law on Tax Act 299 and some Laws. The law proposal we're talking about is that the minimum wage will be exempted from income and stamp tax and all employees' revenues will be taxed as much as the minimum wage, and the incentive for renewable energy production, the extension of VAT exceptions to defence industry projects, the removal of BOTAS debts at the price of deployment, the provision of general and private budgetary administrations in 2021, as well as the payment of revenues from general and private budgetary funds. The arrangements are all important and will comfort our nation, but the main backbone of the proposal is the provisions for minimum wage. The minimum wage in Turkey is determined by a trilateral commission, consisting of workers, employers and states, guaranteed by the Constitution, established legal infrastructure under the Employment Law and implementation regulations. In accordance with the principle of the social state, Article 55 of the Constitution, it is estimated that the state will take the necessary measures to obtain a fair wage in accordance with the work of employees, and that the living conditions and the economic situation of the country will be taken into consideration for those who work for the minimum wage. During the minimum wage assessments, the issue of non-taxion from minimum wage was one of the main issues of working life. The tax burden on minimum wage, on the one hand, reduces the earnings of low incomes, and on the other hand, increases the cost of employers. Therefore, cutbacks on minimum wage have created one of the major problems of labour and employers' unions concerning minimum wage. The minimum fee was agreed to be a net $4,253 for 2022 with the alliance of the parties, while the minimum wage was also adopted as a tax-free tax on income and stamp. In addition to an increase in over 50 per cent of public expectations, the decision to tax the minimum wage was a historic step, and this historic step was taken at the will of the Cumhur Alliance. As the National Movement Party, we have sincerely foreseen that our election statements will not be taxed on minimum wage, and that the minimum wage of all employees' income will be left untaxed, and we have shared our commitments with the public. And during the negotiations, we brought them up again. Therefore, I wish that the arrangement was historically important and that we could express our satisfaction once again, and that it would be good for all our employees. I would like to thank our President, the employees and employers, the government, and all those who have done all the work. Thus, one issue that has long been discussed but has not been concluded in working life has been permanently resolved with a reformist step; the worker, the officer, has provided additional income increase to all our employees. In the proposal, the 10 kilowatts condition, envisioned as a limit of exemption to the power of solar power plants established on the roof and front of the dwellings, is estimated to be 20 kilowatts. The production of electricity where electricity is consumed is an important gain in preventing loss and escape. Turkey's growing demand for energy has become very important because of its safe reception with renewable energy sources, including solar energy, and its contribution to protecting the environment, reducing external dependency and increasing employment. The rise in demand, supply constraints, and climatic causes have led to increased prices of gas up to 10 times worldwide. In addition to the domestic price price of natural gas, the price of domestic gas is set at the most reasonable level, with the protection of consumers, especially households, and the financial outlook of the public enterprise that provides the services being considered; the price increase is partially reflected to our citizens. This affects the financial situation of BOTAS and makes it difficult to manage cash. The envisioned arrangement envisions the release of the company, which ensures that BOTAS's cash management can be carried out in a healthy manner, as well as any unpaid tax, funds and shares, and administrative fines, in exchange for the payment of debt from the treasury, which is related to the increase of delay and delay interest. However, we also consider that it would be appropriate for the policy of pricing natural gas to take into account the amount of consumption and the number of variables similar to regional differences to the social tariff of different pricing.
